**Sweet and Sour Tokwa (Tofu) Recipe**

Ingredients:
- 500g firm tofu (tokwa)
- 1/2 cup sugar
- 1/2 cup vinegar
- 2 tbsp soy sauce
- 2 cloves garlic, minced
- 1 small onion, thinly sliced
- 2 tbsp cornstarch mixed with 2 tbsp water
- 2 tbsp vegetable oil
- Salt and pepper to taste
- Chopped green onions for garnish (optional)

Instructions:
1. Press the tofu to remove excess moisture. Cut into bite-sized cubes.
2. In a bowl, mix together sugar, vinegar, soy sauce, minced garlic, and sliced onion. Set aside.
3. Heat vegetable oil in a wok or large pan over medium-high heat. Add the tofu cubes and stir-fry for about 3-4 minutes until lightly browned.
4. Pour in the sugar and vinegar mixture, stirring constantly. Cook for 2-3 minutes until the sauce thickens slightly.
5. Add the cornstarch mixture and cook for another 1-2 minutes until the sauce becomes glossy and thick.
6. Season with salt and pepper to taste.
7. Garnish with chopped green onions (if using) and serve hot.
